  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the main caller mismatch now is between step 8's save flow and step 11's renderer. the editor updates `posts.body` on autosave/save but only runs `syncembeds` on publish, while `postdetail` is supposed to resolve `::art` / `::resource` / `::media` tokens through `post_media` and `post_assets`; for an already published post, that lets the live body and the join tables diverge after an edit.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: there is also still a caller-level count mismatch on the owner's profile. step 12 says the owner-facing `/:username/posts` tab should call `useposts({ memberid: profilememberid, includedrafts: isownprofile })`, which includes drafts for the owner, but the same step keeps `useuserprofile.postcount` on the public-only `status='published' and admin_status != 'hidden'` predicate. on an own profile with drafts, the badge and the visible card count would still disagree.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-015",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: i checked `/2rp` callers around `handlecreateclick` and `authactionmodal`. today every caller only passes `'art'` or `'resource'`, and the modal label branch only handles those two values, so widening the union for posts also requires updating the supporting caller/ui branch or the new post cta will fall through to the wrong copy.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-034",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the existing caller surface is still the markdown submit page. `src/pages/submitpost/index.tsx:212-350` already calls `createdraft`, `savedraft`, `syncembeds`, and `publishpost`, and the plan only says bundle upload will live as a sub-component or stopgap route without spelling out how those existing callers branch once a post is `render_mode='bundle'`. as written, the same post can still be reopened in the markdown editor and continue writing `body` / embed links unless the caller ui is explicitly changed.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-035",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the preview url return contract is underspecified for current callers. `publishpost` is where a slug is persisted today (`src/lib/posts.ts:90-108`), but bundle uploads can happen against drafts that still have `slug = null`; the plan says `process-bundle` returns `/posts/<slug>?preview=<bv>` without naming the helper or query that produces a stable slug for unpublished posts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-045",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: onshipped callback semantics are now spelled out in step 13.3: `handlevibeshipped(_result: { bundleversionid: string })`. plan chose not to navigate to /posts/<slug> (explicit rationale: post is in pending review, has no slug until approve). that side-steps the slug-ambiguity concern from onshipped-slug-1. sound choice, but see the correctness finding above: the `refreshpost()` in the body is not a real helper, so the chosen flow (stay on submitpost, let isexistingbundlepost flip via refetch) doesn't actually work without additional wiring. addresses the slug side but opens a refresh-mechanism side.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-vibe-mode-for-bundle-20260421-0421"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-052",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual scaled-track caller in `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:54-85`. the wrapper does not call a bare `visualclip`; it calls `visualclipsequence`, and that sequence object is what carries `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(clip, fps)` into remotion. step 3.11 in the plan changes that caller contract to `<absolutefill ...><visualclip .../></absolutefill>`, so it no longer handles the timing arguments the real caller relies on.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-of-a-4-sprint-plan-20260421-0520"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-063",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: flagged that the live creation caller still expects more than the bridge provides: `usetimelineslist().createtimeline()` inserts `project_id`, `user_id`, `name`, `config`, and `asset_registry`, while the planned helper only emits an `update` for an existing row id.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-4-final-of-a-4-sprint-20260421-0928"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-064",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: flagged that the runtime asset-loading caller contract still does not line up, because `loadtimelinejsonfromprovider()` routes registry files through `resolveasseturl()`, which does not handle the tool-generated local path values the bridge preserves verbatim.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-4-final-of-a-4-sprint-20260421-0928"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "post-ship-it-refresh-path-is-missing": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-046",
      "concern": "post-ship-it refresh path is missing: step 13.3's `handlevibeshipped` body calls `refreshpost()` to flip `isexistingbundlepost` via a usepost refetch, but src/hooks/usepost.ts (lines 7-15 `usepostresult` interface, line 372 return) does not export any refetch/refresh helper \u2014 the fetch is a one-shot `useeffect` keyed on `[slugorid]` (l206). without a refetch, `existingpost.render_mode` stays stale after ship it, `isexistingbundlepost` never becomes true, and the auto-switch effect at src/pages/submitpost/index.tsx:559-563 never fires. the ui would remain in vibe mode on a post whose server state is now a shipped bundle. fix requires one of: (a) add a `refetch: () => void` export to usepost (small shipped-file change, probably the cleanest and useful outside vibe too), (b) in handlevibeshipped, call `setmode('bundle')` directly and accept stale `isexistingbundlepost` until the next full mount, or (c) navigate to the same url with a cache-bust param to force usepost remount. plan must commit to one and name the exact code change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-vibe-mode-for-bundle-20260421-0421"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "id": "DEBT-065",
      "concern": "reigh sql handoff: `--print-sql` defaults `--timeline-id` to `<name>` even though `public.timelines.id` is a uuid column, so the emitted default `update` statement is not runnable against the checked-in schema without manual correction.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: posts are broader than just new pages and hooks in this codebase. `src/hooks/useuserprofile.ts` currently computes only `artcount` and `resourcecount`, so the proposed posts tab cannot show a correct count without extending that hook and its loading/error flow; the plan mentions the tab surface, but not the underlying profile-count model it depends on.",

        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the new `/posts/:slug` route also affects shared layout behavior beyond routing. `src/layouts/mainlayout.tsx` currently treats `/2rp`, `/resources/*`, profile pages, `/art/*`, `/submit/*`, and `/auth/*` as dark/resource-context routes, but `/posts/*` is absent, so the plan leaves the top-level post detail page outside the established theme and header rules.",

      ]

    {
      "id": "DEBT-009",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: i also checked the legacy username-path repair in `src/lib/routing.ts`. it only rewrites malformed `/:username/art...` and `/:username/resources...` links for unencoded `#` usernames, so adding `/:username/posts` is another symptom of the same routing surface and needs to be covered too.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the live posts schema is broader than the plan's revised rls sweep. `20260325070000_create_posts.sql` also defines `post_models` with `create policy \"public read post_models\" ... using (true)`, but step 2 only replaces policies on `posts`, `post_media`, and `post_assets`. if any legacy or future post uses `post_models`, draft-linked model associations remain publicly enumerable even though the stated rls goal is to prevent reading other users' drafts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-037",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: there is still a schema-level dependency gap against the actual repository. the committed posts migration in `supabase/migrations/20260325070000_create_posts.sql:4-12` only creates `id`, `member_id`, `title`, `body`, `slug`, `status`, `created_at`, and `updated_at`, while the plan assumes `posts.admin_status`, `posts.cover_media_id`, and `posts.published_at` already exist and only adds `render_mode` plus `active_bundle_version_id`. unless another migration lands first, this bundle plan is not self-contained against the checked-in schema.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-044",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: post-ship state refresh: the missing refetch on `usepost` is a broader symptom. the repo's existing `bundleupload.tsx` likely has a similar re-render gap after its own successful upload (verified by prior critiques that noted `loadlatestbundle` inside bundleupload rather than relying on usepost refetch). vibe's ship it reuses the same server path but lacks a drop-in equivalent of bundleupload's internal re-fetch logic. plan should explicitly cite how bundleupload refreshes its own view after a successful upload and apply the same pattern, or extend usepost with a refetch (benefits both surfaces). either way, this isn't a vibe-only gap \u2014 it's a pattern the repo has had to solve before.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-vibe-mode-for-bundle-20260421-0421"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-049",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: searched the sibling audio renderer and the same playback-rate contract is broader than the visual path that was just corrected. the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/audiotrack.tsx:29-53` computes `playbackrate = getsanitizedplaybackrate(clip.speed)` and passes it into the audio component, but step 3.10 in `plan_v6.md` reduces `src/audiotrack.tsx` to `<audio src={fileurl} {...computemediatrim(clip, fps)} volume={effectivevolume} />` with no `playbackrate`. that means the plan only partially ports the per-clip speed behavior required for sprint 2.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-of-a-4-sprint-plan-20260421-0520"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-059",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the reigh integration problem is broader than probe order. the editor loads timeline data through `loadtimelinejsonfromprovider(provider, timelineid)`, which calls `provider.loadtimeline(timelineid)` and `provider.loadassetregistry(timelineid)` (`reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-data.ts:388-402`), so the real integration boundary is a provider-backed timeline row, not a filesystem drop target. treating the problem as 'find the right folder under `public/`' narrows the issue incorrectly and misses the live persistence layer.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-4-final-of-a-4-sprint-20260421-0928"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-060",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: i searched `cut.py` for `args.shots` and found it is only used to annotate `metadata.sources[primary_asset].shots_ref` (`tools/cut.py:468-472`); it is not consumed by selection, timeline construction, or rendering. that means the proposed always-on `shots.py` stage adds ffmpeg work and files to the default pipeline, but in the current repo it does not change the picks, `hype.timeline.json`, or `hype.mp4` outputs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

> DEVELOPER

You are already running inside the megaplan harness for this step. Do the requested planning/review/execution work directly. Do NOT invoke the `megaplan` CLI, do NOT read or activate the `megaplan` skill, do NOT start nested megaplan plans, and do NOT recurse into another planning harness. Treat mentions of megaplan in the repository or environment as implementation context only.

You are an independent reviewer. Critique the plan against the actual repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/tools





        Idea:
Build three unified deliverables: (1) quality_zones.py ingest stage writing audio_dead + video_dead zones per source, wired into pipeline; (2) refactor refine.py into multi-reviewer with audio_boundary (existing) + visual_quality + speaker_flow + overlay_fit, splitting output into auto_fixes vs flags; (3) new inspect_cut.py CLI rendering SCRIPT + STRUCTURE zoomed-out and per-clip zoom-in with warnings and fix options. All three share a common enriched-arrangement data model. Scope: no arrangement schema changes beyond adding optional warning fields, no Remotion changes, no new LLM call sites, stdlib-only visualizer.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: quality_zones + multi-reviewer refine + inspect_cut (v3)

## Overview

**Root-cause check.** Of the 12 open flags, one group strongly suggests a simplification (not a wrong target): `FLAG-007` + `all_locations` + `scope` all converge on the fact that adding `warnings: list[str]` to `ArrangementClip` (a) has no producer or consumer in the rest of the plan — reviewer output lives in `refine.json` and `inspect_cut.py` reads that file, (b) regenerates `tools/remotion/src/types.generated.ts` via `tools/scripts/gen_remotion_types.py`, violating the "no Remotion changes" constraint, and (c) ripples through `test_arrange.py`/`test_multitrack_cut.py`/`test_hype_cut_invariants.py` without earning its keep. The brief says "no arrangement schema changes **beyond** adding optional warning fields" — that *permits* the change, it does not *require* it. Dropping the field collapses three flags at once and keeps the arrangement schema fully untouched. **v3 removes the schema change entirely** and carries all reviewer output in `refine.json` only. The rest is line-level fixes.

**Idea (unchanged):**
1. `tools/quality_zones.py` — ingest stage emitting `audio_dead` + `video_dead` zones per source, wired into the pipeline.
2. Refactor `tools/refine.py` into a multi-reviewer (`audio_boundary` existing, `visual_quality`, `speaker_flow`, `overlay_fit` new) with `auto_fixes` vs `flags` split.
3. `tools/inspect_cut.py` — stdlib-only visualizer (SCRIPT + STRUCTURE zoomed-out, per-clip zoom-in).
4. All three consume one shared `EnrichedArrangement` data model.

**Constraints (from brief):** no arrangement schema changes (v3 *tightens* this — we add nothing, not even optional warnings); no Remotion changes; no new LLM call sites; stdlib-only visualizer.

**Settled decisions carried from v2:** shared model in `tools/enriched_arrangement.py`; ffmpeg `silencedetect`/`blackdetect` via subprocess; `inspect_cut.py` read-only stdlib; `overlay_fit` flag-only, reuses `cut.py`'s `_MIN_OVERLAY_COVERAGE_SEC`/`_MAX_VISUAL_HOLD_RATIO`; `speaker_flow` keys on `pool_entry.speaker`; metadata in `cut.py`/`refine.py`/`_tool_fingerprints()`; baselined test gate; `quality_zones.py` is a subprocess CLI.

**v3 tightening (addresses the 12 open flags):**
- **No `warnings[]` field.** No `tools/timeline.py` edit. No Remotion regeneration. No arrangement-roundtrip test churn. (Resolves `scope`, `FLAG-007`, `all_locations`.)
- **`overlay_fit` drops `overlay_same_source_as_audio`** (dead code — `pool_d_*` and `pool_v_*` are always distinct per `tools/pool_build.py:113-128`). Reviewer emits only `overlay_insufficient_coverage`. (Resolves `correctness-1`, `FLAG-005`.)
- **`quality_zones.py` honors caller-supplied `--out`** matching the contract of `scenes.py`/`shots.py`/`transcribe.py` at `tools/pipeline.py:322-346`. Pipeline passes the per-source path as `--out`. (Resolves `callers-1`, `FLAG-006`.)
- **`quality_zones_ref` scoped to the assets actually analyzed** (`args.video`/`args.audio`, which both resolve to `main` in the current pipeline). Not a multi-asset dict. Extra `--asset KEY=PATH` values are documented as out of analysis scope until/unless `pipeline.py` grows a per-asset analyzer loop. (Resolves `callers-2`.)
- **STRUCTURE view reads zones from per-asset `quality_zones.json` via `EnrichedArrangement`**, not `hype.timeline.json`. (Resolves `correctness-2`, `FLAG-008`.)
- **`inspect_cut.py` exits 0 regardless of flag count.** CI gating is not in scope. (Resolves `issue_hints-2`.)
- **Smoke criterion reworded** — requires *any* deterministic reviewer finding on the known brief run (the existing `refine.json` already shows `mid_sentence_end` on clip 2, so at minimum `audio_boundary` reproduces that). No claim about `validation.json`'s 8 `clip_a_*` similarity failures, which are unrelated to the new reviewers. (Resolves `issue_hints-1`.)

## Phase 1: Foundation — audit + shared data model

### Step 1: Audit + capture test baseline (`tools/pipeline.py`, `tools/refine.py`, `tools/cut.py`, `tools/timeline.py`, `tools/pool_build.py`, `tools/transcribe.py`, `tools/scenes.py`, `tools/shots.py`, `tools/tests/`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Read** the exact regions the gate cited and pin specifics for later steps:
   - `tools/pipeline.py:16-52,299-351,322-346,349-444,434-578,748-772` — `STEP_ORDER`, `PER_SOURCE_SENTINELS`, stage invocation (explicit `--out` rooted at `args.out`), `--from`/`--skip`, resume, log routing.
   - `tools/cut.py:503-590,614-618,677-852,689-724,887-980,1338-1344` — metadata builder, overlay policy constants, `build_multitrack_timeline()`, `build_metadata_from_arrangement()` call site, audio/visual source kind enforcement.
   - `tools/refine.py:243-250,398-425,401-414` — `_regen_args()` ref plumbing, metadata regeneration, existing iteration/convergence loop.
   - `tools/pool_build.py:113-128` — `pool_d_*`/`pool_v_*` minting; `speaker` optional field.
   - `_tool_fingerprints()` — grep across `tools/` to confirm definition location and current entries.
   - Live pool shape: `runs/ados_pool/pool.json` — confirm `speaker` field presence per dialogue entry.
2. **Capture** the test baseline: `pytest tools/tests -q > .megaplan/baseline.txt` from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/` (not committed). Extract the **set of currently-failing nodeids**. This is the reference set Step 10 diffs against. Pre-existing failures stay out of scope.
3. **Confirm** the four assumptions the gate left untouched (ffmpeg available, stdlib-only visualizer, no new LLM, no multi-asset analysis expected). Stop and raise if any is wrong.

### Step 2: Shared data model (`tools/enriched_arrangement.py` — new)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Create** `tools/enriched_arrangement.py` (stdlib only: `dataclasses`, `pathlib`, `json`, `typing`). Exports:
   - Dataclasses: `EnrichedArrangement`, `EnrichedClip`, `QualityZone`, `ReviewerFinding`, `ReviewerResult`.
   - Enums: `ZoneKind` (`audio_dead` | `video_dead`), `FindingSeverity` (`auto_fix` | `flag`).
   - `EnrichedClip` carries resolved pool entries so reviewers can read `pool_entry.speaker` without re-loading the pool.
2. **Implement** `load(run_dir: Path) -> EnrichedArrangement` that reads: `arrangement.json` (via `timeline.load_arrangement()` — **no writes**, no schema change), `hype.timeline.json` if present, the per-asset `quality_zones.json` pointed to by `hype.metadata.json["pipeline"]["sources"][asset]["quality_zones_ref"]`, the asset-keyed transcript and scenes caches, and the pool. Joins on asset-key (`entry['asset']`, defaulting to `"main"`).
3. **Implement** `apply_auto_fixes(enriched, findings)` as a pure in-memory mutator. Persistence of arrangement changes flows through the existing `timeline.save_arrangement()` — reviewer output itself is written into `refine.json` by `refine.py`, **not into `arrangement.json`**.
4. **Keep** under ~300 lines.

## Phase 2: Ingest stage + pipeline/metadata wiring

### Step 3: `quality_zones.py` CLI (`tools/quality_zones.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `tools/quality_zones.py` as a subprocess CLI mirroring `tools/scenes.py`/`tools/shots.py`. Required arguments match sibling stages: `<source_path>` and `--out <path>`. **The caller-supplied `--out` is authoritative** — whatever path `pipeline.py` passes is the file the stage writes, exactly like `scenes.py`/`shots.py` (resolving FLAG-006/callers-1).
2. **Detect** `audio_dead` zones via `ffmpeg -i <src> -af silencedetect=n=-35dB:d=0.5 -f null -` (parse stderr). `video_dead` zones via `ffmpeg -vf blackdetect=d=0.3:pic_th=0.98 -f null -`. Thresholds exposed as `--silence-db`, `--silence-dur`, `--black-dur`, `--black-pic-th` flags with those defaults.
3. **Write** to the caller-supplied `--out` with shape:
   ```json
   {"source_sha256": "…", "asset_key": "main", "zones": [{"kind": "audio_dead", "start": 12.3, "end": 13.0}, …]}
   ```
   `asset_key` records the logical asset the source is analyzed for; current pipeline only calls on `main`. Cache is content-keyed on `source_sha256`: on hit, re-emit the cached zones without re-running ffmpeg.
4. **Expose** an internal `compute(source_path: Path) -> QualityZonesReport` for unit tests only. The subprocess CLI remains the pipeline integration point (resolving callers-3 in v2; reaffirmed here).

### Step 4: Pipeline stage registration (`tools/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Register** `"quality_zones"` in `STEP_ORDER` at `tools/pipeline.py:16-52` between `"scenes"` and `"shots"`.
2. **Add** `"quality_zones"` to `PER_SOURCE_SENTINELS` with sentinel `quality_zones.done`.
3. **Extend** the stage invocation at `tools/pipeline.py:299-351,322-346` to enqueue `quality_zones.py` per source (same call pattern as `scenes.py`): pass `<source_path>` plus an explicit `--out <path>` rooted at `args.out`. Ensure `--from quality_zones` / `--skip quality_zones` validate at `:434-578`, resume works at `:349-444`, and per-brief/per-source log and sentinel routing at `:748-772` is extended. This is the complete pipeline.py registration surface per `all_locations-1`.
4. **Scope**: analyzers run on `args.video`/`args.audio` only (current behavior). Extra `--asset KEY=PATH` values are passed to `cut.py` but not to per-source analyzers — `quality_zones.py` does not run on them, and `quality_zones_ref` in metadata is a single entry keyed to the main asset, not a multi-asset dict. If/when per-asset analysis is needed, that is follow-up work (resolves callers-2).
5. **Confirm** `pipeline.py` does not touch metadata — that remains owned by `cut.py`/`refine.py` (Step 5).

### Step 5: Metadata wiring (`tools/cut.py`, `tools/refine.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Update** `_tool_fingerprints()` (grep to locate; evidence points to `tools/cut.py`) to include `"quality_zones.py"`, `"refine.py"`, `"inspect_cut.py"`, and `"enriched_arrangement.py"`.
2. **Extend** `build_metadata_from_arrangement()` at `tools/cut.py:887-980` with a new optional argument `quality_zones_ref: Optional[Path]` (single path — main asset only; no dict), emitting it as `pipeline.sources["main"].quality_zones_ref`. Keyed alongside the existing `scenes_ref`/`transcript_ref`/`shots_ref` contract.
3. **Thread** the new arg through both call sites — `tools/cut.py:1338-1344` and `tools/refine.py:401-414` — and update `tools/refine.py:_regen_args()` at `:243-250` to discover the file at `<run_dir>/../quality_zones.json` (same location pattern as `scenes.json`) and pass the path through.
4. **Add** `"quality_zones"` to `pipeline.steps_run` emission at `tools/cut.py:503-590`.
5. **Update** fixtures in `tools/tests/test_refine.py:160-177` and `tools/tests/test_multitrack_cut.py:173-190` so `build_metadata_from_arrangement()` callers provide the new kwarg (default `None` is accepted for runs that pre-date `quality_zones`).

## Phase 3: Reviewers + refine orchestration

### Step 6: Reviewer protocol + audio_boundary extraction (`tools/reviewers/__init__.py`, `tools/reviewers/audio_boundary.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `tools/reviewers/__init__.py` with the `Reviewer` Protocol:
   ```python
   class Reviewer(Protocol):
       name: str
       def review(self, enriched: EnrichedArrangement) -> list[ReviewerFinding]: ...
   ```
   `ReviewerFinding` fields: `clip_order`, `code`, `severity` (`auto_fix` | `flag`), optional `proposed_patch` (only when `severity=auto_fix`), `message`.
2. **Extract** existing `refine.py` logic into `tools/reviewers/audio_boundary.py` preserving issue codes `mid_sentence_end`, `mid_sentence_start`, `boilerplate_start`, `no_valid_nudge` and the per-clip output shape (`trim_before`/`trim_after`/`similarity_*`/`source_transcript_text_*`).
3. **Keep** <250 lines.

### Step 7: Three new reviewers (`tools/reviewers/visual_quality.py`, `tools/reviewers/speaker_flow.py`, `tools/reviewers/overlay_fit.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **`visual_quality.py`**: for each clip, compute the visual trim range on the timeline and find intersections with the asset's `video_dead` zones (from the enriched model, which pulled them from per-asset `quality_zones.json` in Step 2). Emit `flag` `visual_dead_overlap` when overlap ≥ `max(0.5s, 15% of clip duration)`. No `auto_fix` path.
2. **`speaker_flow.py`**: iterate arrangement dialogue clips in order; for each consecutive pair on the audio track, read each clip's `pool_entry.speaker` (via the enriched model). Emit `flag` `speaker_repeat_without_break` when two adjacent dialogue clips have the **same non-None speaker** AND no intervening b-roll stinger AND non-contiguous `trim_sub_range`. Emit nothing when either speaker is `None`.
3. **`overlay_fit.py`**: flag-only. For each `overlay`-role clip, emit `flag` `overlay_insufficient_coverage` when the overlay clip duration `< _MIN_OVERLAY_COVERAGE_SEC` OR the implied hold ratio exceeds `_MAX_VISUAL_HOLD_RATIO` (both imported from `tools/cut.py`). **The `overlay_same_source_as_audio` check proposed in v2 is dropped** — `pool_d_*` and `pool_v_*` ids are distinct by construction (`tools/pool_build.py:113-128`), so the comparison is always `False` on valid arrangements; the v3 reviewer has exactly one code.
4. **Keep** each module <250 lines.

### Step 8: Refactor `refine.py` orchestration (`tools/refine.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Rewrite** the top level of `tools/refine.py` to:
   - Build `EnrichedArrangement` via `enriched_arrangement.load()`.
   - Instantiate the four reviewers and run them per iteration.
   - Apply `auto_fix` findings (only `audio_boundary` emits any) — up to 3 passes like today.
   - Persist arrangement changes through the existing `timeline.save_arrangement()` **without any schema change**.
   - Write `refine.json` with top-level keys:
     ```json
     {
       "iterations_run": 3,
       "converged": false,
       "auto_fixes": {"audio_boundary": [...legacy per_clip shape...]},
       "flags": {"visual_quality": [...], "speaker_flow": [...], "overlay_fit": [...]},
       "rejected_nudges": [...],
       "per_clip": [...legacy mirror for back-compat...]
     }
     ```
     `per_clip` is a mirror of `auto_fixes["audio_boundary"]` so pre-existing readers (tests, older runs) stay green.
2. **Keep** `_regen_args()` and metadata regeneration (updated in Step 5) working.
3. **Update** `tools/tests/test_refine.py` for: all four reviewers run, split correctness, legacy `per_clip` still present, `audio_boundary` idempotence on a second pass, `speaker_flow` emits nothing when adjacent speakers are `None`.

## Phase 4: Visualizer + test gate

### Step 9: `inspect_cut.py` (`tools/inspect_cut.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `tools/inspect_cut.py`: stdlib-only (`argparse`, `json`, `pathlib`, `textwrap`, `sys`; ANSI via plain escape strings — no `rich`/`click`/`curses`/`blessed`). Entry: `python -m tools.inspect_cut <run_dir> [--clip N] [--no-color] [--json]`.
2. **SCRIPT view** (default): reading-order dialogue transcript sourced from `EnrichedArrangement`, with visual-stinger placeholders between dialogue clips. Inline warnings rendered as `⚠ [code] message` under each affected clip. Warning data comes from `refine.json` (`flags` + `auto_fixes`) via `EnrichedArrangement` — **not from `arrangement.json`** (no schema change there).
3. **STRUCTURE view**: proportional ASCII timeline across `a1`/`v1`/`v2` tracks. Quality-zone shading (`░` for `audio_dead`, `▒` for `video_dead`) is overlaid on the relevant track using the per-asset zones already loaded into `EnrichedArrangement` from `quality_zones.json` (via the `quality_zones_ref` metadata added in Step 5). `hype.timeline.json` contributes the clip geometry only; zone data is NOT read from it (resolves correctness-2/FLAG-008).
4. **Zoom-in** (`--clip N`): all reviewer findings for that clip, before/after transcript, trim range, zone overlaps, and an informational "fix options" block mirroring any `auto_fix` `proposed_patch`. Read-only.
5. **Exit code 0** unless the tool itself errors. Flag presence does **not** change exit code (resolves issue_hints-2). CI gating is out of scope; if needed later, can be bolted on via `--strict` without changing default behavior.
6. **Keep** under ~400 lines.

### Step 10: Test gate (`tools/tests/test_quality_zones.py`, `tools/tests/test_enriched_arrangement.py`, `tools/tests/test_inspect_cut.py` — new; extend `test_refine.py`, `test_pipeline_caching.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Run** cheap-first in this order (all from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/`):
   - `pytest tools/tests/test_enriched_arrangement.py tools/tests/test_quality_zones.py -x`
   - `pytest tools/tests/test_refine.py -x`
   - `pytest tools/tests/test_inspect_cut.py -x`
   - `pytest tools/tests/test_pipeline_caching.py -x`
2. **Whole-suite regression diff**: run `pytest tools/tests -q` and compare the failing-nodeids set to the Step 1 baseline. **Gate: post-change failing set ⊆ baseline failing set** (zero new regressions). Pre-existing failures stay out of scope per `FLAG-004`.
3. **No arrangement-schema-roundtrip test churn**: because v3 drops the `warnings` field, `test_arrange.py`, `test_arrangement_schema.py`, `test_multitrack_cut.py`, and `test_hype_cut_invariants.py` should pass unchanged (any movement in their baseline indicates accidental scope creep — investigate).
4. **Manual smoke**: from the known brief run `runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief`:
   - `python -m tools.pipeline --resume …` completes and produces a non-empty `quality_zones.json` for the main asset.
   - The new `refine.json` still contains `mid_sentence_end` on clip 2 (preserved by `audio_boundary` extraction) under `auto_fixes.audio_boundary`.
   - `python -m tools.inspect_cut runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief` renders SCRIPT + STRUCTURE + `--clip 2` zoom and exits 0. It emits at least one deterministic reviewer entry (at minimum the `audio_boundary` `mid_sentence_end` finding that today's `refine.json` already contains). No claim is made about tying new reviewer flags to `validation.json`'s 8 similarity-based failures — those are unrelated.

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 (audit + baseline) — pre-req for everything.
2. Step 2 (shared data model) — consumed by all downstream modules.
3. Step 3 (quality_zones CLI).
4. Step 4 (pipeline registration).
5. Step 5 (metadata wiring) — depends on Step 3 producing the cache file and Step 4 wiring it in.
6. Step 6 (reviewer protocol + audio_boundary extraction).
7. Step 7 (three new reviewers) — depends on 2, 3, 6.
8. Step 8 (refine orchestration) — depends on 2, 5, 6, 7.
9. Step 9 (inspect_cut) — depends on 2, 8.
10. Step 10 (tests) — extended alongside each step; run as the final gate.

## Validation Order
1. **Cheap early**: `pytest tools/tests/test_enriched_arrangement.py test_quality_zones.py` after Steps 2 and 3.
2. **Biggest risk**: `pytest tools/tests/test_refine.py` after Step 8.
3. **Inspector**: `pytest tools/tests/test_inspect_cut.py` after Step 9.
4. **Full-suite regression diff** against Step 1 baseline: `pytest tools/tests -q` — no new failing nodeids.
5. **Manual smoke**: `python -m tools.pipeline --resume …` + `python -m tools.inspect_cut runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief`.

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-004",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-005",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-006",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the inline video plan does not fit the existing media pipeline. `mediauploader` only returns `file` objects, and the current upload flow writes a public supabase url into `cloudflare_thumbnail_url`; meanwhile every existing video surface (`src/pages/artdetail/index.tsx`, `src/pages/resourcedetail/index.tsx`, and `src/pages/resources/hlsplayer.tsx`) expects `cloudflare_playback_hls_url` for playable video, so uploaded videos would not render through `hlsplayer` as planned.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-029",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed rpc auth path is internally inconsistent. `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role`, but its body authorizes with `auth.uid()` and `public.is_admin()`. in the current repo, edge functions authenticate the bearer separately with `supabase.auth.getuser(accesstoken)` and then run db work as service role (`supabase/functions/cloudflare-stream-ingest/index.ts:43-72`), so this sql function would not automatically see the caller's `auth.uid()` unless the plan adds a second authenticated client or rewrites the guard.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-030",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the storage/versioning sequence still has a race. step 3 uploads files under `post-bundles/bundles/<post_id>/<next_version>/...` before calling `register_bundle_version(...)`, but step 1 also makes the rpc compute `version = max(version) + 1` itself. concurrent uploads can therefore diverge between storage prefix and db version, and any rpc failure after upload leaves orphaned files because storage writes happened before the transactional sql step.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-050",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared step 3.11 in `plan_v6.md` with the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:66-85`. the plan's scaled visual branch wraps a bare `<visualclip .../>` in `<absolutefill style={{transform: scale(...)}}>` but does not include a surrounding `<sequence from=... durationinframes=...>`. in the reference, that branch wraps `<visualclipsequence .../>`, which is where clip timing is applied, so implementing the plan literally would make scaled non-position-override clips mount from frame 0 for the full composition instead of honoring `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(...)`.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-of-a-4-sprint-plan-20260421-0520"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-058",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed cache contract for the `cut` step is technically unsafe because it uses only `hype.timeline.json` as the sentinel even though `cut.py` writes `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and `hype.metadata.json` as one unit (`tools/cut.py:763-769`). later planned steps consume the extra outputs as required inputs (`tools/render_remotion.py:62-99` requires `--assets`, and the bridge copies `hype.assets.json`), so a partial prior run or manual deletion of assets/metadata would still be treated as 'cached' and cause downstream breakage.",

      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: supporting infrastructure miss: the post-ship refetch path. the plan's step 13.3 acknowledges the flow ('triggers a post refetch so isexistingbundlepost auto-switches') but the referenced `refreshpost()` helper doesn't exist in src/hooks/usepost.ts. this is a supporting-infrastructure gap: either usepost needs a new `refetch` export (one small edit to a shipped file) or handlevibeshipped needs a different mechanism. plan should name the exact change to src/hooks/usepost.ts if option (a) is chosen.",

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the main caller mismatch now is between step 8's save flow and step 11's renderer. the editor updates `posts.body` on autosave/save but only runs `syncembeds` on publish, while `postdetail` is supposed to resolve `::art` / `::resource` / `::media` tokens through `post_media` and `post_assets`; for an already published post, that lets the live body and the join tables diverge after an edit.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: there is also still a caller-level count mismatch on the owner's profile. step 12 says the owner-facing `/:username/posts` tab should call `useposts({ memberid: profilememberid, includedrafts: isownprofile })`, which includes drafts for the owner, but the same step keeps `useuserprofile.postcount` on the public-only `status='published' and admin_status != 'hidden'` predicate. on an own profile with drafts, the badge and the visible card count would still disagree.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-015",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: i checked `/2rp` callers around `handlecreateclick` and `authactionmodal`. today every caller only passes `'art'` or `'resource'`, and the modal label branch only handles those two values, so widening the union for posts also requires updating the supporting caller/ui branch or the new post cta will fall through to the wrong copy.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-034",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the existing caller surface is still the markdown submit page. `src/pages/submitpost/index.tsx:212-350` already calls `createdraft`, `savedraft`, `syncembeds`, and `publishpost`, and the plan only says bundle upload will live as a sub-component or stopgap route without spelling out how those existing callers branch once a post is `render_mode='bundle'`. as written, the same post can still be reopened in the markdown editor and continue writing `body` / embed links unless the caller ui is explicitly changed.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-035",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the preview url return contract is underspecified for current callers. `publishpost` is where a slug is persisted today (`src/lib/posts.ts:90-108`), but bundle uploads can happen against drafts that still have `slug = null`; the plan says `process-bundle` returns `/posts/<slug>?preview=<bv>` without naming the helper or query that produces a stable slug for unpublished posts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-045",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: onshipped callback semantics are now spelled out in step 13.3: `handlevibeshipped(_result: { bundleversionid: string })`. plan chose not to navigate to /posts/<slug> (explicit rationale: post is in pending review, has no slug until approve). that side-steps the slug-ambiguity concern from onshipped-slug-1. sound choice, but see the correctness finding above: the `refreshpost()` in the body is not a real helper, so the chosen flow (stay on submitpost, let isexistingbundlepost flip via refetch) doesn't actually work without additional wiring. addresses the slug side but opens a refresh-mechanism side.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-vibe-mode-for-bundle-20260421-0421"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-052",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual scaled-track caller in `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:54-85`. the wrapper does not call a bare `visualclip`; it calls `visualclipsequence`, and that sequence object is what carries `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(clip, fps)` into remotion. step 3.11 in the plan changes that caller contract to `<absolutefill ...><visualclip .../></absolutefill>`, so it no longer handles the timing arguments the real caller relies on.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-of-a-4-sprint-plan-20260421-0520"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-063",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: flagged that the live creation caller still expects more than the bridge provides: `usetimelineslist().createtimeline()` inserts `project_id`, `user_id`, `name`, `config`, and `asset_registry`, while the planned helper only emits an `update` for an existing row id.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-4-final-of-a-4-sprint-20260421-0928"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-064",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: flagged that the runtime asset-loading caller contract still does not line up, because `loadtimelinejsonfromprovider()` routes registry files through `resolveasseturl()`, which does not handle the tool-generated local path values the bridge preserves verbatim.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-4-final-of-a-4-sprint-20260421-0928"
      ]
    }

  "post-ship-it-refresh-path-is-missing": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-046",
      "concern": "post-ship-it refresh path is missing: step 13.3's `handlevibeshipped` body calls `refreshpost()` to flip `isexistingbundlepost` via a usepost refetch, but src/hooks/usepost.ts (lines 7-15 `usepostresult` interface, line 372 return) does not export any refetch/refresh helper \u2014 the fetch is a one-shot `useeffect` keyed on `[slugorid]` (l206). without a refetch, `existingpost.render_mode` stays stale after ship it, `isexistingbundlepost` never becomes true, and the auto-switch effect at src/pages/submitpost/index.tsx:559-563 never fires. the ui would remain in vibe mode on a post whose server state is now a shipped bundle. fix requires one of: (a) add a `refetch: () => void` export to usepost (small shipped-file change, probably the cleanest and useful outside vibe too), (b) in handlevibeshipped, call `setmode('bundle')` directly and accept stale `isexistingbundlepost` until the next full mount, or (c) navigate to the same url with a cache-bust param to force usepost remount. plan must commit to one and name the exact code change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-vibe-mode-for-bundle-20260421-0421"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "id": "DEBT-037",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: there is still a schema-level dependency gap against the actual repository. the committed posts migration in `supabase/migrations/20260325070000_create_posts.sql:4-12` only creates `id`, `member_id`, `title`, `body`, `slug`, `status`, `created_at`, and `updated_at`, while the plan assumes `posts.admin_status`, `posts.cover_media_id`, and `posts.published_at` already exist and only adds `render_mode` plus `active_bundle_version_id`. unless another migration lands first, this bundle plan is not self-contained against the checked-in schema.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-044",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: post-ship state refresh: the missing refetch on `usepost` is a broader symptom. the repo's existing `bundleupload.tsx` likely has a similar re-render gap after its own successful upload (verified by prior critiques that noted `loadlatestbundle` inside bundleupload rather than relying on usepost refetch). vibe's ship it reuses the same server path but lacks a drop-in equivalent of bundleupload's internal re-fetch logic. plan should explicitly cite how bundleupload refreshes its own view after a successful upload and apply the same pattern, or extend usepost with a refetch (benefits both surfaces). either way, this isn't a vibe-only gap \u2014 it's a pattern the repo has had to solve before.",

      {
        "id": "DEBT-004",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-005",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-006",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the inline video plan does not fit the existing media pipeline. `mediauploader` only returns `file` objects, and the current upload flow writes a public supabase url into `cloudflare_thumbnail_url`; meanwhile every existing video surface (`src/pages/artdetail/index.tsx`, `src/pages/resourcedetail/index.tsx`, and `src/pages/resources/hlsplayer.tsx`) expects `cloudflare_playback_hls_url` for playable video, so uploaded videos would not render through `hlsplayer` as planned.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-029",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed rpc auth path is internally inconsistent. `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role`, but its body authorizes with `auth.uid()` and `public.is_admin()`. in the current repo, edge functions authenticate the bearer separately with `supabase.auth.getuser(accesstoken)` and then run db work as service role (`supabase/functions/cloudflare-stream-ingest/index.ts:43-72`), so this sql function would not automatically see the caller's `auth.uid()` unless the plan adds a second authenticated client or rewrites the guard.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-030",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the storage/versioning sequence still has a race. step 3 uploads files under `post-bundles/bundles/<post_id>/<next_version>/...` before calling `register_bundle_version(...)`, but step 1 also makes the rpc compute `version = max(version) + 1` itself. concurrent uploads can therefore diverge between storage prefix and db version, and any rpc failure after upload leaves orphaned files because storage writes happened before the transactional sql step.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-050",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared step 3.11 in `plan_v6.md` with the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:66-85`. the plan's scaled visual branch wraps a bare `<visualclip .../>` in `<absolutefill style={{transform: scale(...)}}>` but does not include a surrounding `<sequence from=... durationinframes=...>`. in the reference, that branch wraps `<visualclipsequence .../>`, which is where clip timing is applied, so implementing the plan literally would make scaled non-position-override clips mount from frame 0 for the full composition instead of honoring `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(...)`.",

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed cache contract for the `cut` step is technically unsafe because it uses only `hype.timeline.json` as the sentinel even though `cut.py` writes `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and `hype.metadata.json` as one unit (`tools/cut.py:763-769`). later planned steps consume the extra outputs as required inputs (`tools/render_remotion.py:62-99` requires `--assets`, and the bridge copies `hype.assets.json`), so a partial prior run or manual deletion of assets/metadata would still be treated as 'cached' and cause downstream breakage.",

(Resolves callers-2 / FLAG-011.)

   - The pool at `runs/<pool>/pool.json`.
   Joins on asset-key (`entry['asset']`, default `"main"`).
3. **Implement** `apply_auto_fixes(enriched, findings)` as a pure in-memory mutator. Persistence flows through the existing `timeline.save_arrangement()`; reviewer output itself goes into `refine.json` written by `refine.py`.
4. **Keep** under ~300 lines.

## Phase 2: Ingest stage + pipeline/metadata wiring

### Step 3: `quality_zones.py` CLI (`tools/quality_zones.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `tools/quality_zones.py` as a subprocess CLI mirroring `tools/scenes.py`/`tools/shots.py`. Arguments: positional `<source_path>` plus `--out <path>`; **caller-supplied `--out` is authoritative** (matches `tools/pipeline.py:322-346` contract).
2. **Detect** `audio_dead` zones via `ffmpeg -i <src> -af silencedetect=n=-35dB:d=0.5 -f null -` (parse stderr `silence_start=` / `silence_end=`). `video_dead` zones via `ffmpeg -vf blackdetect=d=0.3:pic_th=0.98 -f null -` (parse `black_start` / `black_end`). Thresholds exposed as `--silence-db`, `--silence-dur`, `--black-dur`, `--black-pic-th` with those defaults.
3. **Write** to the caller-supplied `--out`:
   ```json
   {"source_sha256": "…", "asset_key": "main", "zones": [{"kind": "audio_dead", "start": 12.3, "end": 13.0}, …]}
   ```
   Content-keyed cache on `source_sha256`: on hit, re-emit the cached payload without re-running ffmpeg.
4. **Expose** an internal `compute(source_path: Path) -> QualityZonesReport` for unit tests only; the subprocess CLI remains the integration point.

### Step 4: Pipeline stage registration (`tools/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Register** `"quality_zones"` in `STEP_ORDER` between `"scenes"` and `"shots"`.
2. **Add** `"quality_zones.json"` (the artifact filename — **not** `quality_zones.done`) to `PER_SOURCE_SENTINELS`, matching the existing convention (`transcript.json`, `scenes.json`, `shots.json`, etc.). This resolves all_locations / FLAG-009.
3. **Wire** `build_pool_steps()` to enqueue `quality_zones.py` per source, mirroring `scenes.py`: pass `<source_path>` plus an explicit `--out <runs/<pool>/quality_zones.json>` rooted at `args.out` (the pool root, sibling of `scenes.json`). Ensure `--from quality_zones` / `--skip quality_zones` validate, `sentinel_paths()` returns `quality_zones.json`, resume logic skips when the sentinel exists, and per-brief/per-source log routing includes the new stage.
4. **Update** `tools/tests/test_pipeline_caching.py` fixtures to seed/assert `quality_zones.json` alongside the other per-source artifacts.
5. **Scope** (explicit): per-source analyzers run on `args.video`/`args.audio` only (both resolve to `main` today). Extra `--asset KEY=PATH` values are passed to `cut.py` only, not to per-source analyzers; `quality_zones_ref` is a single path for `main`, not a multi-asset dict. Multi-asset analysis is out of scope for this plan.

### Step 5: Metadata wiring (`tools/cut.py`, `tools/refine.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Update** `_tool_fingerprints()` (location pinned in Step 1) to include `"quality_zones.py"`, `"refine.py"`, `"inspect_cut.py"`, and `"enriched_arrangement.py"`.
2. **Extend** `build_metadata_from_arrangement()` (`tools/cut.py:887-980`, writes at `:559-563` and `:954-958`) to accept an optional `quality_zones_ref: Optional[Path]` argument and emit it at **top-level `sources[primary_asset].quality_zones_ref`** — the same container that already holds `scenes_ref`/`transcript_ref`/`shots_ref`. **Not** `pipeline.sources`. (Resolves FLAG-002 / correctness.)
3. **Thread** the new arg through both call sites — `tools/cut.py:1338-1344` and `tools/refine.py:401-414` — plus `tools/refine.py:_regen_args()` at `:243-250`. In `_regen_args()`, **read the existing `scenes_ref` (or `transcript_ref`) from `sources.main` in the existing metadata, take its parent directory, and append `quality_zones.json`** to find the zone file. Do NOT compute `<run_dir>/../quality_zones.json` — that resolves to the wrong directory because brief dirs live at `runs/<pool>/briefs/<brief>/` while source artifacts live at `runs/<pool>/` (two levels up, not one). This resolves callers-1 / FLAG-010.
4. **Add** `"quality_zones"` to `pipeline.steps_run` emission in `tools/cut.py:503-590`.
5. **Update** fixtures in `tools/tests/test_refine.py:160-177` and `tools/tests/test_multitrack_cut.py:173-190` so `build_metadata_from_arrangement()` callers provide the new kwarg (default `None` accepted for runs predating the stage).

## Phase 3: Reviewers + refine orchestration

### Step 6: Reviewer protocol + audio_boundary extraction (`tools/reviewers/__init__.py`, `tools/reviewers/audio_boundary.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `tools/reviewers/` as a regular directory. Because the parent `tools/` is not a package (Step 1 confirmed), reviewers import each other via the same sibling pattern — Step 1 pins the exact form. `tools/reviewers/__init__.py` exists only to define the `Reviewer` Protocol:
   ```python
   class Reviewer(Protocol):
       name: str
       def review(self, enriched: EnrichedArrangement) -> list[ReviewerFinding]: ...
   ```
   `ReviewerFinding`: `clip_order`, `code`, `severity` (`auto_fix` | `flag`), optional `proposed_patch` (only when `severity=auto_fix`), `message`.
2. **Extract** existing `tools/refine.py` logic into `tools/reviewers/audio_boundary.py`, preserving issue codes `mid_sentence_end`, `mid_sentence_start`, `boilerplate_start`, `no_valid_nudge` and the per-clip output shape (`trim_before`/`trim_after`/`similarity_*`/`source_transcript_text_*`) observed in today's `runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief/refine.json`.
3. **Keep** <250 lines.

### Step 7: Three new reviewers (`tools/reviewers/visual_quality.py`, `tools/reviewers/speaker_flow.py`, `tools/reviewers/overlay_fit.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **`visual_quality.py`**: for each clip, compute its visual trim on the timeline and intersect with the asset's `video_dead` zones (loaded by `EnrichedArrangement` from `sources.main.quality_zones_ref`). Emit `flag` `visual_dead_overlap` when overlap ≥ `max(0.5s, 15% of clip duration)`. No `auto_fix` path. If the enriched model has no zones for the asset (missing ref or missing file), the reviewer emits nothing.
2. **`speaker_flow.py`**: iterate arrangement dialogue clips in order; for each consecutive pair on the audio track, read each clip's `pool_entry.speaker` via the enriched model. Emit `flag` `speaker_repeat_without_break` when two adjacent dialogue clips share the **same non-None speaker** AND have no intervening b-roll stinger AND their `trim_sub_range` values are non-contiguous. Emit nothing when either speaker is `None`.
3. **`overlay_fit.py`**: flag-only. For each `overlay`-role clip, emit `flag` `overlay_insufficient_coverage` when the overlay clip duration `< _MIN_OVERLAY_COVERAGE_SEC` OR the implied hold ratio exceeds `_MAX_VISUAL_HOLD_RATIO` (both sourced from `tools/cut.py` per Step 1's audit — either imported directly or via a one-line public-constant lift).
4. **Keep** each module <250 lines.

### Step 8: Refactor `refine.py` orchestration (`tools/refine.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Rewrite** the top level of `tools/refine.py` to:
   - Build `EnrichedArrangement` via `enriched_arrangement.load()`.
   - Instantiate the four reviewers and run them per iteration.
   - Apply `auto_fix` findings (only `audio_boundary` emits any) up to 3 passes like today.
   - Persist arrangement changes through the existing `timeline.save_arrangement()` — **no schema change**.
   - Write `refine.json`:
     ```json
     {
       "iterations_run": 3,
       "converged": false,
       "auto_fixes": {"audio_boundary": [...legacy per_clip shape...]},
       "flags": {"visual_quality": [...], "speaker_flow": [...], "overlay_fit": [...]},
       "rejected_nudges": [...],
       "per_clip": [...legacy mirror of auto_fixes.audio_boundary for back-compat...]
     }
     ```
2. **Keep** `_regen_args()` and metadata regeneration (updated in Step 5) working through the new flow.
3. **Update** `tools/tests/test_refine.py` for: all four reviewers run, split correctness, legacy `per_clip` still present, `audio_boundary` idempotence on second pass, `speaker_flow` emits nothing when adjacent speakers are `None`.

## Phase 4: Visualizer + test gate

### Step 9: `inspect_cut.py` (`tools/inspect_cut.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `tools/inspect_cut.py`: stdlib-only (`argparse`, `json`, `pathlib`, `textwrap`, `sys`; ANSI via plain escape strings — no `rich`/`click`/`curses`). Entry: invokable **either** `python tools/inspect_cut.py <run_dir>` **or** `python -m tools.inspect_cut <run_dir>` depending on Step 1's pinned style. Flags: `--clip N`, `--no-color`, `--json`.
2. **SCRIPT view**: reading-order dialogue transcript from `EnrichedArrangement`, with visual-stinger placeholders between dialogue clips. Inline warnings rendered under each affected clip (source: `refine.json` via `EnrichedArrangement`; **not** `arrangement.json` — schema is unchanged).
3. **STRUCTURE view**: proportional ASCII timeline across `a1`/`v1`/`v2` tracks. Clip geometry from `hype.timeline.json`. Quality-zone shading (`░` `audio_dead`, `▒` `video_dead`) overlaid using per-asset zones the enriched model loaded from `sources.main.quality_zones_ref`.
4. **Zoom-in** (`--clip N`): reviewer findings, before/after transcript, trim range, zone overlaps, and an informational "fix options" block mirroring any `auto_fix` `proposed_patch`. Read-only.
5. **Exit code 0** unless the tool itself errors. Flag count does not affect exit code.
6. **Keep** under ~400 lines.

### Step 10: Test gate (`tools/tests/test_quality_zones.py`, `tools/tests/test_enriched_arrangement.py`, `tools/tests/test_inspect_cut.py` — new; extend `test_refine.py`, `test_pipeline_caching.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Run** cheap-first from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/`:
   - `pytest tools/tests/test_enriched_arrangement.py tools/tests/test_quality_zones.py -x`
   - `pytest tools/tests/test_refine.py -x`
   - `pytest tools/tests/test_inspect_cut.py -x`
   - `pytest tools/tests/test_pipeline_caching.py -x`
2. **Whole-suite regression diff**: `pytest tools/tests -q --tb=no` and compare failing nodeids to the Step 1 baseline. **Gate: post-change failing set ⊆ Step 1 baseline failing set** (zero new regressions).
3. **Baseline-red exclusions** (explicitly listed, gate-verified pre-existing failures): the "pass unchanged" gate for arrangement-roundtrip tests applies to every nodeid in `test_arrange.py`, `test_arrangement_schema.py`, `test_multitrack_cut.py`, `test_hype_cut_invariants.py` **except**:
   - `tools/tests/test_arrange.py::ArrangeTest::test_cli_fills_hash_envelope_from_file_bytes`
   - `tools/tests/test_arrange.py::ArrangeTest::test_payload_passes_validate_arrangement`
   - `tools/tests/test_hype_cut_invariants.py::test_hype_cut_invariants_on_discovered_run_dirs`
   plus any additional red nodeids captured in the Step 1 baseline. These stay red both before and after. (Resolves FLAG-004 / issue_hints.)
4. **Manual smoke** on `runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief`:
   - `python tools/pipeline.py --resume …` (or `-m`, per Step 1's pinned invocation form) completes and produces a non-empty `runs/ados_pool/quality_zones.json` for the main asset.
   - `hype.metadata.json` now contains `sources.main.quality_zones_ref` pointing at that file, and `pipeline.steps_run` lists `"quality_zones"`.
   - `refine.json` still contains `mid_sentence_end` on clip 2 under `auto_fixes.audio_boundary`.
   - `python tools/inspect_cut.py runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief` renders SCRIPT + STRUCTURE + `--clip 2` zoom, exits 0, and surfaces at least one deterministic reviewer entry (`mid_sentence_end` already satisfies this from today's `refine.json`).

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 — audit + baseline capture is a hard pre-req; do not start Step 2 until the audit matches the gate-verified facts above.
2. Step 2 — shared data model.
3. Step 3 — `quality_zones.py` CLI.
4. Step 4 — pipeline registration (depends on Step 3's CLI contract).
5. Step 5 — metadata wiring (depends on Step 3 producing the cache file and Step 4 passing `--out` correctly).
6. Step 6 — reviewer protocol + `audio_boundary` extraction.
7. Step 7 — three new reviewers (depends on Steps 2, 3, 6).
8. Step 8 — refine orchestration (depends on Steps 2, 5, 6, 7).
9. Step 9 — `inspect_cut.py` (depends on Steps 2, 8).
10. Step 10 — tests; extended alongside each step and run as the final gate.

## Validation Order
1. **Cheap early**: `pytest tools/tests/test_enriched_arrangement.py test_quality_zones.py` after Steps 2 and 3.
2. **Biggest risk**: `pytest tools/tests/test_refine.py` after Step 8.
3. **Inspector**: `pytest tools/tests/test_inspect_cut.py` after Step 9.
4. **Regression diff** against Step 1 baseline: `pytest tools/tests -q --tb=no` — failing-nodeid set ⊆ baseline.
5. **Manual smoke**: pipeline resume on `runs/ados_pool/briefs/brief` + `inspect_cut.py` render.

        Known accepted debt grouped by subsystem:
        {
  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-004",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 8 still makes draft creation happen only through the 800ms autosave path, while the publish flow immediately calls `syncembeds(postid, ...)` and `publishpost(postid, ...)`. if an author types content and clicks publish before the first autosave has created the row, there is no guaranteed `postid` for the primary publish path, so the plan has a runtime hole in the initial create-and-publish flow.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-005",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the cover fetch path is internally inconsistent. step 8 stores the selected cover on `posts.cover_media_id`, but step 5 says `usepost` fetches the post row plus `post_media` and `post_assets` and then 'resolves cover from `post.cover_media_id`'; because the cover picker is separate from body embeds and the plan never says to also link the chosen cover into `post_media`, a cover that is not embedded in the body would need its own direct media fetch or it will not be renderable.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-006",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the inline video plan does not fit the existing media pipeline. `mediauploader` only returns `file` objects, and the current upload flow writes a public supabase url into `cloudflare_thumbnail_url`; meanwhile every existing video surface (`src/pages/artdetail/index.tsx`, `src/pages/resourcedetail/index.tsx`, and `src/pages/resources/hlsplayer.tsx`) expects `cloudflare_playback_hls_url` for playable video, so uploaded videos would not render through `hlsplayer` as planned.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "build-a-user-authored-posts-20260420-1310"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-029",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed rpc auth path is internally inconsistent. `register_bundle_version(...)` is granted only to `service_role`, but its body authorizes with `auth.uid()` and `public.is_admin()`. in the current repo, edge functions authenticate the bearer separately with `supabase.auth.getuser(accesstoken)` and then run db work as service role (`supabase/functions/cloudflare-stream-ingest/index.ts:43-72`), so this sql function would not automatically see the caller's `auth.uid()` unless the plan adds a second authenticated client or rewrites the guard.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-030",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the storage/versioning sequence still has a race. step 3 uploads files under `post-bundles/bundles/<post_id>/<next_version>/...` before calling `register_bundle_version(...)`, but step 1 also makes the rpc compute `version = max(version) + 1` itself. concurrent uploads can therefore diverge between storage prefix and db version, and any rpc failure after upload leaves orphaned files because storage writes happened before the transactional sql step.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "design-the-bundle-mode-posts-20260420-2034"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-050",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared step 3.11 in `plan_v6.md` with the vendored `tools/_reference/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/compositions/timelinerenderer.tsx:66-85`. the plan's scaled visual branch wraps a bare `<visualclip .../>` in `<absolutefill style={{transform: scale(...)}}>` but does not include a surrounding `<sequence from=... durationinframes=...>`. in the reference, that branch wraps `<visualclipsequence .../>`, which is where clip timing is applied, so implementing the plan literally would make scaled non-position-override clips mount from frame 0 for the full composition instead of honoring `clip.at` and `getclipdurationinframes(...)`.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-of-a-4-sprint-plan-20260421-0520"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-058",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the proposed cache contract for the `cut` step is technically unsafe because it uses only `hype.timeline.json` as the sentinel even though `cut.py` writes `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and `hype.metadata.json` as one unit (`tools/cut.py:763-769`). later planned steps consume the extra outputs as required inputs (`tools/render_remotion.py:62-99` requires `--assets`, and the bridge copies `hype.assets.json`), so a partial prior run or manual deletion of assets/metadata would still be treated as 'cached' and cause downstream breakage.",

    },

      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the repository also has legacy username repair in `normalizelegacyhashusernamepath()` that only recognizes `/art` and `/resources`. adding `/:username/posts` without extending that helper leaves one of the existing routing safety nets incomplete for post urls.",

      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the plan's hard-coded `frame-ancestors https://banodoco.com https://www.banodoco.com` is not enough supporting infrastructure for this repo as written. the validation section explicitly expects staging verification, but a fixed production-only ancestor list would prevent embedding from local or staging hosts and there is no existing site-origin constant in the checked-in frontend to keep those values in sync.",

      "id": "DEBT-047",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: supporting infrastructure miss: the post-ship refetch path. the plan's step 13.3 acknowledges the flow ('triggers a post refetch so isexistingbundlepost auto-switches') but the referenced `refreshpost()` helper doesn't exist in src/hooks/usepost.ts. this is a supporting-infrastructure gap: either usepost needs a new `refetch` export (one small edit to a shipped file) or handlevibeshipped needs a different mechanism. plan should name the exact change to src/hooks/usepost.ts if option (a) is chosen.",
